dlon=lon2-lon1\n    a=sin(dlat/2)**2+cos(lat1)*cos(lat2)*sin(dlon/2)**2\n    return 2*R*asin(sqrt(a))\n# distance matrix\nids=list(meta.index)\ndist=pd.DataFrame(index=ids,columns=ids,dtype=float)\nfor i in ids:\n for j in ids:\n  dist.loc[i,j]=hav(meta.loc[i,'latitude'],meta.loc[i,'longitude'],meta.loc[j,'latitude'],meta.loc[j,'longitude'])\n\ndef peer_ids(sid, mode='district'):\n    p=pol[sid]; d=meta.loc[sid,'district']\n    candidates=[x for x in ids if x\"'!=sid and pol[x]==p]\n    if mode=='\"'district':\n        ds=[x for x in candidates if meta.loc[x,'district']==d]\n        if len(ds)>=2: return ds\n    if mode=='nearest5': return list(dist.loc[sid,candidates].sort_values().head(5).index)\n    if mode=='nearest8': return list(dist.loc[sid,candidates].sort_values().head(8).index)\n    return candidates\n\ndef baseline(sid, peers):\n    return Y[peers].mean(axis=1, skipna=True).values.astype(float)\n\nrows=[]\nfor sid in ids:\n y=Y[sid].values.astype(float)\n for mode in ['district','nearest5','nearest8','all']:\n  peers=peer_ids(sid,mode)\n  b=baseline(sid,peers)\n  lf=linfit_resid(y,b)\n  c0=corr(y,b)\n  rms=rmse(y,b)\n  std=np.nanstd(y, ddof=1)\n  bstd=np.nanstd(b, ddof=1)\n  # correlation lag: compare y_t with b_{t-lag}? lags -4..4. corr(a,b):\n    a=np.asarray(a,float); b=np.asarray(b,float)\n    m=np.isfinite(a)&np.isfinite(b)\n    if m.sum()<6: return np.nan, m.sum()\n    if np.nanstd(a[m])==0 or np.nanstd(b[m])==0: return np.nan, m.sum()\n    return np.corrcoef(a[m],b[m])[0,1], m.sum()\n\ndef residual(y,b):\n    a=np.asarray(y,float); bb=np.asarray(b,float)\n    m=np.isfinite(a)&np.isfinite(bb)\n    if m.sum()<6: return np.full_like(a,np.nan), (np.nan,np.nan,np.nan)\n    X=np.vstack([np.ones(m.sum()),bb[m]]).T\n    coef=np.linalg.lstsq(X,a[m],rcond=None)[0]\n    res=np.full_like(a,np.nan)\n    res[m]=a[m] - X@coef\n    ss=np.sum((a[m]-a[m].mean())**2); r2=1-np.sum(res[m]**2)/ss if ss>0 else np.nan\n    return res,(coef[0],coef[1],r2)\n\ndef peer_ids(sid, mtype):\n    candidates=[x for x in ids if
